# PROFILE_MEDIA_DATABASE_V76B_ROADMAP_STATUS

## Current state after V76B
The project has crossed from exploratory planning into an implementable backend architecture for Profile/Media Database mode.

## Completed backend layers
- V75A database foundation
- V75B source planning
- V75C case path planning
- V75D review queue planning
- V75E reviewed folder operation guard layer
- V75F manifest/tree preview helpers
- V75G corrected Database/Profiles and Cases layout
- V75H UI-neutral database view model foundation
- V75I through V75O sidebar DATABASE toggle and square visual mode behavior
- V75P persisted runtime DATABASE/FILES mode
- V75Q case workspace model
- V75R source intake records
- V75S profile intake records
- V75T case manifest model
- V75U guarded case materialization model
- V75V batch case JSON model
- V75W database index
- V75X backend database search
- V75Y main Database-mode view model
- V75Z session, saved views, review report, and guarded export planning
- V76A dashboard, navigation targets, and workbench bundle
- V76B full integration regression, safety invariant audit, and readiness report

## What V76B proves
- Batch JSON can drive the read model.
- Index/search/mode/session/workbench layers compose successfully.
- Source-chain gap, disputed framing, unknown role, and parser-warning review lanes are present.
- Export planning remains dry-run by default.
- Wrong export confirmations are blocked.
- The default regression path does not perform folder scans, folder creation, moves, renames, file copies, file writes, media downloads, automatic classification, or sensitive identifier inference.

## Ready for implementation
The next major implementation pack can begin wiring the main GUI Database workbench panel.

## Still pending
- real GUI panel rendering
- real database/project selector
- import assistant for user-created case/source/profile batch JSON
- existing-folder-to-batch planner
- controlled materialize/apply workflow
- controlled reviewed rename/move workflow
- source-role vocabulary alias alignment before final user-facing labels

## GUI rule to preserve
The sidebar remains only a mode switch: DATABASE On/Off and FILES. It must not regain the old preview/filter behavior.

## Suggested next pack
V76C — Main GUI Database workbench panel.
